[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Determine the number of eggs Janet eats in a day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Janet eats 3 eggs for breakfast every morning.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - She eats 4 eggs for baking muffins every day.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total number of eggs she eats in a day is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the number of eggs Janet sells at the farmers' market:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total number of eggs laid by the ducks is 16.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The number of eggs she eats in a day is 7.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the number of eggs she sells at the farmers' market is:

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.  They normally order 2 meals that cost $5 each and a $5 bottle of wine.  How much do they make a week if they are open 2 days a week? Let's think step by step and output the final answer after "####".
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m assistant
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m [response] To determine how much Tom's restaurant makes in a week, we need to calculate the total number of reservations and the total revenue from these reservations. Here's the step-by-step process:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 1. **Calculate the total number of reservations per night:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the total number of reservations per week:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Since the restaurant is open 2 days a week, the total number of reservations per week is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      6 \text{ reservations/day} \times 2 \text{ days/week} = 12 \text{ reservations/week}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the total revenue from reservations:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant normally orders 2 meals that cost $5 each and a $5 bottle of wine.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total cost for the meals is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      2 \text{ meals} \times \$5/\text{meal} = \$10
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total cost for the wine is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      1 \text{ bottle} \times \$5/\text{bottle} = \$5
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total revenue from reservations is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$10 + \$5 = \$15
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 4. **Calculate the total weekly revenue:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total weekly revenue is the total revenue from reservations multiplied by the number of reservations per week:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$15 \times 12 \text{ reservations/week} = \$180
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m assistant
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m [response] To determine how much Janet makes at the farmers' market every day, we need to follow these steps:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 1. **Calculate the total number of eggs laid by the ducks in a day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Janet's ducks lay 16 eggs per day.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the total number of eggs Janet eats in a day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Janet eats 3 eggs for breakfast.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - She eats 4 muffins for baking.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total number of eggs she eats in a day is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      3 \text{ (breakfast)} + 4 \text{ (baking)} = 7 \text{ eggs}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the number of eggs Janet sells in a day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total number of eggs laid by the ducks is 16.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The number of eggs she eats in a day is 7.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the number of eggs she sells in a day is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      16 \text{ (total)} - 7 \text{ (eaten)} = 9 \text{ eggs}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 4. **Calculate the total revenue Janet makes at the farmers' market:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Janet sells each egg for $2.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The number of eggs she sells in a day is 9.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total revenue is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      9 \text{ eggs} \times 2 \text{ dollars/egg} = 18 \text{ dollars}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m user
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.  They normally order 2 meals that cost $5 each and a $5 bottle of wine.  How much do they make a week if they are open 2 days a week? Let's think step by step and output the final answer after "####".
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m assistant
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m [response] To determine how much Tom's restaurant makes in a week, we need to calculate the total revenue from reservations and the revenue from wine orders separately, and then sum these amounts.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 1. **Calculate the revenue from reservations:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - They normally order 2 meals that cost $5 each and a $5 bottle of wine.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The cost of one meal is $5, so the cost of 2 meals is \(2 \times 5 = \$10\).
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The cost of one bottle of wine is $5, so the cost of 1 bottle of wine is \(1 \times 5 = \$5\).
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total revenue from reservations is \(10 + 5 = \$15\).
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the revenue from wine orders:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant normally orders 2 bottles of wine each night.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The cost of one bottle of wine is $5.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total revenue from wine orders is \(2 \times 5 = \$10\).
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the total revenue for the week:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from reservations is $15.

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the total number of eggs Janet eats in a day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Janet eats 3 eggs for breakfast.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - She eats 4 muffins for baking.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total number of eggs she eats in a day is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the number of eggs Janet sells in a day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total number of eggs laid by the ducks is 16.

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m assistant
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m [response] To determine how much Tom's restaurant makes in a week, we need to calculate the total number of reservations and the total revenue from these reservations. Here's the step-by-step process:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 1. **Calculate the total number of reservations per night:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the total number of reservations per week:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant is open 2 days a week.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total number of reservations per week is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      6 \text{ reservations/day} \times 2 \text{ days/week} = 12 \text{ reservations/week}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the total revenue from reservations:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant normally orders 2 meals that cost $5 each and a $5 bottle of wine.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total cost for the meals is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      2 \text{ meals} \times \$5/\text{meal} = \$10
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total cost for the wine is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      1 \text{ bottle} \times \$5/\text{bottle} = \$5
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total revenue from reservations is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$10 + \$5 = \$15
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 4. **Calculate the total weekly revenue:**

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the total number of eggs Janet eats in a day:**

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m assistant
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m [response] To determine how much Tom's restaurant makes in a week, we need to calculate the total number of reservations and the total revenue from these reservations. Here's the step-by-step process:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 1. **Calculate the total number of reservations per night:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant is open 2 days a week.

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      6 \text{ reservations/day} \times 2 \text{ days/week} = 12 \text{ reservations/week}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the total revenue from reservations:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant normally orders 2 meals that cost $5 each and a $5 bottle of wine.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total cost for the meals is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      2 \text{ meals} \times \$5/\text{meal} = \$10
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total cost for the wine is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      1 \text{ bottle} \times \$5/\text{bottle} = \$5
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total revenue from reservations is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$10 + \$5 = \$15
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 4. **Calculate the total weekly revenue:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from reservations is $15.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant is open 12 nights a week.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the total weekly revenue is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$15 \text{ per night} \times 12 \text{ nights/week} = \$180
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m #### Conclusion:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m #### $#### 180
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m So, the total amount Tom's restaurant makes in a week is **$180**.

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the total revenue per week:**

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the total number of eggs Janet eats in a day:**

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the number of eggs Janet sells in a day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total number of eggs laid by the ducks is 16.

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m [response] To determine how much Tom's restaurant makes in a week, we need to calculate the total revenue from reservations and the revenue from wine orders separately, and then sum these amounts.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 1. **Calculate the total revenue from reservations:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - They normally order 2 meals that cost $5 each.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - They also order a $5 bottle of wine.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from reservations per night is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      6 \text{ reservations} \times 2 \text{ meals} \times \$5 \text{ per meal} = 6 \times 2 \times 5 = \$60
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Since they are open 2 days a week, the total revenue from reservations per week is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$60 \times 2 = \$120
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the total revenue from wine orders:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The restaurant orders a $5 bottle of wine each time they order a meal.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - They order 2 meals per reservation.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from wine orders per reservation is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$5 \text{ per meal} \times 2 \text{ meals} = \$10
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \$10 \times 2 = \$20
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the total revenue per week:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from reservations is \$120.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from wine orders is \$20.

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m assistant
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m [response] To determine how much Tom's restaurant makes in a week, we need to calculate the total revenue from both the reservations and the wine orders. Let's break it down step by step.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 1. **Calculate the number of reservations per day:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Tom's restaurant gets 6 reservations a night.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - They are open 2 days a week.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Therefore, the number of reservations per week is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      6 \text{ reservations/night} \times 2 \text{ nights/week} = 12 \text{ reservations/week}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 2. **Calculate the revenue from reservations:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - Each reservation costs $5.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from reservations per week is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      12 \text{ reservations/week} \times 5 \text{ dollars/reservation} = 60 \text{ dollars/week}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 3. **Calculate the revenue from wine orders:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - They normally order 2 meals that cost $5 each.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - They also order a $5 bottle of wine.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from wine orders per week is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      2 \text{ meals} \times 5 \text{ dollars/meal} + 5 \text{ dollars}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      = 10 \text{ dollars} + 5 \text{ dollars} = 15 \text{ dollars/week}
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \]
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 4. **Calculate the total revenue per week:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total revenue from both reservations and wine orders is:
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      60 \text{ dollars/week} + 15 \text{ dollars/week} = 75 \text{ dollars/week}

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m 5. **Determine the weekly profit:**
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The weekly profit is the total revenue minus the total cost.
[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m    - The total cost is the sum of the cost of the meals and the cost of the wine:

[36m(TaskRunner pid=570916)[0m      \[
